- [ ] **Step 5: 寫 `herdr-plugin.toml`**

```toml
# herdr-plugin.toml — manifest for herdr-scm.
#
# A read-only, multi-repo source-control overview: every git repo under the current herdr
# workspace, its branch and ahead/behind, its changed files, and a diff for the selected one.
# The panel appears ONLY in response to an explicit action (its keybinding) — there are no
# event hooks and no automatic invocation.
#
# v1 is Linux-only: no macOS/Windows build script and no `-windows` action ids. `platforms`
# keeps the field in place for when that changes.

id = "herdr-scm"
name = "herdr-scm"
version = "0.1.0"
description = "A read-only, multi-repo source-control overview panel for the current herdr workspace."
# 0.9.0 is where `herdr pane list --workspace <id>` and the `pane list` payload this plugin
# parses were verified. It is also the version the plugin was developed against.
min_herdr_version = "0.9.0"
platforms = ["linux"]

# Install-time build. v1 compiles from source — no prebuilt download, no network.
[[build]]
command = ["/bin/sh", "scripts/build.sh"]

# The panel. herdr performs the split placement on launch; the actions below summon it.
[[panes]]
id = "scm"
title = "SCM"
placement = "split"
command = ["./target/release/herdr-scm"]

# The action that summons the panel beside the current work.
[[actions]]
id = "open-scm"
title = "Open SCM overview"
description = "Open the read-only multi-repo source-control overview in a split pane."
command = ["bash", "scripts/open-scm.sh"]

# The same panel in its own tab. Idempotent across the tabs of the current workspace: switches
# to an existing panel rather than duplicating it, and toggles off when already on it.
[[actions]]
id = "open-scm-tab"
title = "Open SCM overview (tab)"
description = "Open the source-control overview in its own tab (switch to it if already open)."
command = ["bash", "scripts/open-scm-tab.sh"]
```

- [ ] **Step 6: 寫三個 script**

`scripts/build.sh`：

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# build.sh — the herdr [[build]] step for herdr-scm.
#
# v1 builds from source: no prebuilt download and no network. `~/.cargo/env` is sourced first so
# cargo is found even when herdr was launched without ~/.cargo/bin on PATH (a GUI or login-less
# launch); the `[ -f ]` guard means a missing env file cannot abort the build.
set -eu

[ -f "$HOME/.cargo/env" ] && . "$HOME/.cargo/env"

if ! command -v cargo >/dev/null 2>&1; then
  echo "herdr-scm needs Rust to build, but cargo was not found. Install it from https://rustup.rs, then re-run the install." >&2
  exit 1
fi

exec cargo build --release
```

`scripts/open-scm.sh`：

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Idempotent launcher for the SCM panel — "launch-or-focus, toggle on repeat", scoped to the
# current tab:
#   - no SCM pane in this tab      -> open a split (focused)
#   - an SCM pane exists, unfocused -> focus it
#   - the focused pane IS the panel -> close it (herdr has no hide-without-close, and reopening
#                                      just re-walks the tree — cheap)
#
# The decision is computed in-process by the binary itself (`--launch-decision`, fed the
# `pane list` JSON on stdin), so it is unit-tested and the pane id it returns is already
# validated flag-safe. Any failure degrades to OPEN.
#
# herdr has no focus-by-id, so a focus is a `zoom <id> --on/--off` cycle: `--on` focuses (and
# maximizes), `--off` un-maximizes while keeping focus.
set -uo pipefail

herdr_bin="${HERDR_BIN_PATH:-herdr}"
script_dir="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]:-$0}")" && pwd)"
scm_bin="$script_dir/../target/release/herdr-scm"

open_pane() {
  exec "$herdr_bin" plugin pane open \
    --plugin herdr-scm \
    --entrypoint scm \
    --placement split \
    --direction right \
    --focus
}

decision="OPEN"
if [ -x "$scm_bin" ]; then
  # NOTE: `herdr pane list` takes no `--json` flag on 0.9.0 — it already emits JSON.
  panes="$("$herdr_bin" pane list 2>/dev/null || true)"
  if [ -n "$panes" ]; then
    decision="$(printf '%s' "$panes" | "$scm_bin" --launch-decision 2>/dev/null || echo OPEN)"
  fi
fi

case "$decision" in
  "FOCUS "*)
    pid="${decision#FOCUS }"
    "$herdr_bin" pane zoom "$pid" --on >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    exec "$herdr_bin" pane zoom "$pid" --off
    ;;
  "CLOSE "*)
    exec "$herdr_bin" pane close "${decision#CLOSE }"
    ;;
  *)
    open_pane
    ;;
esac
```

`scripts/open-scm-tab.sh`：與上一個相同，差別只有三處——註解說明是 tab 版、`--launch-decision-tab`、`open_tab()` 用 `--placement tab`（不帶 `--direction`），以及多一個 `SWITCHTAB` 分支：

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Idempotent launcher for the SCM panel in its own TAB — "open-or-switch, toggle on repeat",
# scoped across the tabs of the CURRENT WORKSPACE:
#   - no SCM pane in this workspace        -> open the panel in a new tab (focused)
#   - an SCM pane in another tab here      -> switch to that tab (no duplicate panel)
#   - an SCM pane in this tab, unfocused   -> focus it in place
#   - the focused pane IS the panel        -> close it (herdr auto-closes the emptied tab)
# A panel open in a DIFFERENT workspace is left alone and a fresh one opens here — the action
# reaches this workspace's panel, it never switches you across workspaces.
set -uo pipefail

herdr_bin="${HERDR_BIN_PATH:-herdr}"
script_dir="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]:-$0}")" && pwd)"
scm_bin="$script_dir/../target/release/herdr-scm"

open_tab() {
  exec "$herdr_bin" plugin pane open \
    --plugin herdr-scm \
    --entrypoint scm \
    --placement tab \
    --focus
}

decision="OPEN"
if [ -x "$scm_bin" ]; then
  panes="$("$herdr_bin" pane list 2>/dev/null || true)"
  if [ -n "$panes" ]; then
    decision="$(printf '%s' "$panes" | "$scm_bin" --launch-decision-tab 2>/dev/null || echo OPEN)"
  fi
fi

case "$decision" in
  "SWITCHTAB "*)
    # If the target tab vanished between the snapshot and now, fall back to opening a fresh
    # tab rather than leaving the keypress a silent no-op. (No `exec`, so `||` can run.)
    "$herdr_bin" tab focus "${decision#SWITCHTAB }" || open_tab
    ;;
  "FOCUS "*)
    pid="${decision#FOCUS }"
    "$herdr_bin" pane zoom "$pid" --on >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
    exec "$herdr_bin" pane zoom "$pid" --off
    ;;
  "CLOSE "*)
    exec "$herdr_bin" pane close "${decision#CLOSE }"
    ;;
  *)
    open_tab
    ;;
esac
```

然後：

```bash
chmod +x scripts/build.sh scripts/open-scm.sh scripts/open-scm-tab.sh
```

- [ ] **Step 7: 寫 `config.example.toml`**

```toml
# herdr-scm configuration — copy to $HERDR_PLUGIN_CONFIG_DIR/config.toml
# (find it with: herdr plugin config-dir herdr-scm), or to
# ~/.config/herdr-scm/config.toml.
#
# The file is READ-ONLY to herdr-scm: it is never written back. A parse error degrades the
# WHOLE file to the defaults below rather than failing to start.

# Seconds between status snapshots. 0 turns automatic polling off — press `r` to refresh.
poll_interval_secs   = 3

# Re-walk the filesystem for the repo list every N poll rounds. The walk is the expensive
# half, so it runs far less often than the status query.
rescan_every         = 10

# How many directory levels below each scan start to search for nested repos.
scan_depth           = 4

# Directory names pruned unconditionally, before any gitignore rule is consulted. Setting this
# REPLACES the list; `[]` prunes nothing.
scan_excludes        = ["node_modules", "target", "vendor", ".venv", "dist"]

# The external diff renderer. "" shows plain text. The program receives the patch on stdin and
# its stdout is displayed (after ANSI neutralization).
diff_tool            = "delta"

# At or above this pane width the panel splits left/right (tree 40% / diff 60%); below it the
# panel stacks, tree on top.
split_threshold_cols = 120

# Rebind any action. A value REPLACES that action's default keys and takes the key from
# whichever action held it. `Esc` always closes an overlay regardless of what is set here.
# Action names: nav_down nav_up activate focus_toggle next_change prev_change refresh
#               toggle_all zoom copy_path open_editor help close quit
[keys]
# refresh = "g"
# nav_up  = ["w", "Up"]
```

- [ ] **Step 8: 寫 `tests/manifest.rs`**

```rust
//! The manifest is the contract with herdr; these pin the values other parts of the code and
//! the launcher scripts depend on.

use std::path::Path;

fn manifest() -> toml::Value {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string("herdr-plugin.toml").expect("read herdr-plugin.toml");
    text.parse().expect("parse herdr-plugin.toml")
}

fn array<'a>(manifest: &'a toml::Value, key: &str) -> &'a [toml::Value] {
    manifest[key].as_array().unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{key} array"))
}

#[test]
fn the_manifest_declares_the_ids_the_spec_fixes() {
    let m = manifest();
    assert_eq!(m["id"].as_str(), Some("herdr-scm"));
    assert_eq!(m["min_herdr_version"].as_str(), Some("0.9.0"));
    assert_eq!(
        m["platforms"].as_array().map(Vec::len),
        Some(1),
        "v1 is Linux-only"
    );
    assert_eq!(m["platforms"][0].as_str(), Some("linux"));
}

#[test]
fn the_manifest_version_matches_the_crate_version() {
    // A drifting version makes the installed plugin report a lie.
    assert_eq!(manifest()["version"].as_str(), Some(env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")));
}

#[test]
fn the_pane_entry_matches_what_the_launcher_scripts_ask_for() {
    let m = manifest();
    let pane = &array(&m, "panes")[0];
    assert_eq!(pane["id"].as_str(), Some("scm"));
    assert_eq!(pane["placement"].as_str(), Some("split"));
    assert_eq!(
        pane["command"][0].as_str(),
        Some("./target/release/herdr-scm")
    );
}

#[test]
fn the_pane_title_is_the_label_the_launch_decision_matches_on() {
    // herdr reports the pane's `title` as its `label` in `pane list`; if these drift, the
    // launcher stops recognizing its own panel and opens a second one every keypress.
    assert_eq!(
        manifest()["panes"][0]["title"].as_str(),
        Some(herdr_scm::launch::PANE_LABEL)
    );
}

#[test]
fn both_actions_exist_with_the_ids_the_readme_tells_users_to_bind() {
    let m = manifest();
    let ids: Vec<&str> = array(&m, "actions")
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|a| a["id"].as_str())
        .collect();
    assert_eq!(ids, ["open-scm", "open-scm-tab"]);
}

#[test]
fn every_command_the_manifest_names_exists_on_disk_and_is_executable() {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    let m = manifest();
    let mut scripts: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    for key in ["build", "actions"] {
        for entry in array(&m, key) {
            if let Some(command) = entry["command"].as_array() {
                for arg in command {
                    if let Some(s) = arg.as_str()
                        && s.starts_with("scripts/")
                    {
                        scripts.push(s.to_string());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    assert!(!scripts.is_empty(), "no scripts referenced");
    for script in scripts {
        let path = Path::new(&script);
        assert!(path.exists(), "{script} is referenced but missing");
        let mode = std::fs::metadata(path).expect("metadata").permissions().mode();
        assert!(mode & 0o111 != 0, "{script} is not executable (mode {mode:o})");
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_launcher_scripts_never_pass_the_json_flag_that_herdr_rejects() {
    // `herdr pane list --json` fails with "unknown option" on 0.9.0; the flag would silently
    // reduce every launch to OPEN, duplicating the panel on every keypress.
    for script in ["scripts/open-scm.sh", "scripts/open-scm-tab.sh"] {
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(script).expect(script);
        assert!(
            !text.contains("pane list --json"),
            "{script} passes --json to pane list"
        );
        assert!(text.contains("pane list"), "{script} never lists panes");
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_example_config_documents_every_setting_the_loader_understands() {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string("config.example.toml").expect("config.example.toml");
    for key in [
        "poll_interval_secs",
        "rescan_every",
        "scan_depth",
        "scan_excludes",
        "diff_tool",
        "split_threshold_cols",
        "[keys]",
    ] {
        assert!(text.contains(key), "{key} is undocumented");
    }
}

#[test]
fn the_example_config_still_parses_to_the_built_in_defaults() {
    // Every value in the example is written as the default; if one drifts, users copying the
    // file silently get different behavior from a fresh install.
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string("config.example.toml").expect("config.example.toml");
    let settings = herdr_scm::config::resolve(herdr_scm::config::parse(&text), &|_| None);
    let defaults = herdr_scm::config::Settings::default();
    assert_eq!(settings.poll_interval_secs, defaults.poll_interval_secs);
    assert_eq!(settings.rescan_every, defaults.rescan_every);
    assert_eq!(settings.scan_depth, defaults.scan_depth);
    assert_eq!(settings.scan_excludes, defaults.scan_excludes);
    assert_eq!(settings.diff_tool, defaults.diff_tool);
    assert_eq!(settings.split_threshold_cols, defaults.split_threshold_cols);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: 寫 `tests/cli_smoke.rs`**

```rust
//! The binary's argv surface, exercised as a real process.

use std::io::Write;
use std::process::{Command, Stdio};

fn bin() -> &'static str {
    env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_herdr-scm")
}

#[test]
fn the_version_flag_prints_the_crate_version() {
    let out = Command::new(bin()).arg("--version").output().expect("run");
    assert!(out.status.success());
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
    assert!(text.contains(env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")), "{text}");
}

/// Feed `stdin` to the binary with `flag` and return its trimmed stdout.
fn decision(flag: &str, stdin: &str) -> String {
    let mut child = Command::new(bin())
        .arg(flag)
        .stdin(Stdio::piped())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .spawn()
        .expect("spawn");
    child
        .stdin
        .take()
        .expect("stdin")
        .write_all(stdin.as_bytes())
        .expect("write");
    let out = child.wait_with_output().expect("wait");
    String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).trim().to_string()
}

#[test]
fn the_launch_decision_reads_the_pane_list_from_stdin() {
    let json = r#"{"result":{"panes":[
        {"pane_id":"wA:p1","tab_id":"wA:t1","focused":true},
        {"pane_id":"wA:p2","tab_id":"wA:t1","focused":false,"label":"SCM"}
    ]}}"#;
    assert_eq!(decision("--launch-decision", json), "FOCUS wA:p2");
}

#[test]
fn the_tab_launch_decision_switches_to_a_panel_in_another_tab() {
    let json = r#"{"result":{"panes":[
        {"pane_id":"wA:p1","tab_id":"wA:t1","focused":true},
        {"pane_id":"wA:p9","tab_id":"wA:t2","focused":false,"label":"SCM"}
    ]}}"#;
    assert_eq!(decision("--launch-decision-tab", json), "SWITCHTAB wA:t2");
}

#[test]
fn garbage_on_stdin_degrades_to_open_rather_than_failing_the_launcher() {
    assert_eq!(decision("--launch-decision", "not json"), "OPEN");
    assert_eq!(decision("--launch-decision-tab", ""), "OPEN");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 10: 寫 `README.md`**

至少要包含：一句話說明、螢幕示意（把 spec §5.1 的兩張 ASCII 圖貼上）、安裝步驟、鍵位表（與 `src/input.rs` 的 REGISTRY 一致）、設定說明（指向 `config.example.toml`）、以及一節「What this does NOT do」列出 spec §2 的四個非目標（唯讀、無 graph、單 workspace、Linux only）。安裝步驟：

````markdown
## Install

```bash
git clone <this repo> ~/project/herdr-plugin2
herdr plugin link ~/project/herdr-plugin2
```

Then bind a key in `~/.config/herdr/config.toml`:

```toml
[[keys]]
key = "prefix+g"
action = "open-scm"

[[keys]]
key = "prefix+shift+g"
action = "open-scm-tab"
```

Building needs Rust (https://rustup.rs). After a source change, `cargo build --release` is
enough — the linked plugin picks it up on the next launch.
````

cargo test
cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings && cargo fmt --check
cargo build --release
herdr plugin link /home/bal/project/herdr-plugin2
herdr plugin action invoke open-scm || herdr plugin action list
```
Expected：面板在 split pane 開起來；再按一次綁定的鍵會關掉它（toggle）；`open-scm-tab` 會在獨立 tab 開，且在已開的情況下切過去而非再開一個。

```

- [ ] `cargo clippy --all-targets -- -D warnings` 無輸出
- [ ] `cargo fmt --check` 無差異

- [ ] `grep -rn 'Command::new("git")' src/` 只出現在 `src/git.rs`
- [ ] `grep -rnE '"(add|commit|checkout|stash|reset|push|pull|merge|rebase|clean|rm|mv)"' src/git.rs` 無輸出（唯讀邊界）
- [ ] `grep -rn 'tokio' Cargo.toml` 無輸出
- [ ] 在真的 herdr session 裡：面板開得起來、跨 repo 都列到、選檔看得到 diff、`q` 離開後終端正常

## Self-Review 紀錄

**Spec 覆蓋**（逐節對照）：

| Spec 節 | 由哪個 task 實作 |
|---|---|
| §3.1 掃描起點 | Task 4（context）、Task 5（pane cwds）、Task 7（`scan_roots`）、Task 8（`RealTopLevel`） |
| §3.2 走訪與三層剪枝 | Task 7（`discover::scan` + 真 git fixture 驗證 5 個被 ignore 的 repo） |
| §3.3 repo 種類判定 | Task 2（`repo_kind::classify`）、Task 7（fixture 驗證四種 kind） |
| §3.4 重掃頻率 | Task 13（`Poller` 的 `interval` 與 `rescan_every`） |
| §4 資料模型 | Task 2（`model`）、Task 6（`porcelain` 產生 groups） |
| §4.1 diff 基準 | Task 8（`diff_args`）、Task 12（`prepare_untracked`）、Task 13（`run_job` 分派） |
| §5.1 響應式佈局 | Task 9（`layout::geometry`）、Task 15（`draw`） |
| §5.2 三種列 | Task 15（`repo_line` / `group_line` / `file_line`） |
| §5.3 按鍵 | Task 11（REGISTRY + bindings）、Task 14（意圖處理） |
| §5.4 空狀態 | Task 14（`is_empty` / `scan_roots`）、Task 15（`empty_state_lines`） |
| §5.5 身分式狀態保存 | Task 10（`Tree::rebuild`） |
| §6 模組表 | 檔案結構一節逐一對應 |
| §7 資料流與並行 | Task 13（兩條執行緒 + `catch_unwind`）、Task 14（diff job 序號） |
| §8 設定 | Task 3（`config`） |
| §9 錯誤處理與降級 | Task 8（error/stale 列）、Task 12（上限、二進位、delta 缺席）、Task 7（權限錯誤）、Task 5（herdr 缺席） |
| §10 安全邊界 | Task 8（`GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS` / `--no-ext-diff` / `--no-textconv`）、Task 12（ANSI 中和）、Task 16（OSC 52 base64、editor 純交棒） |
| §11 測試策略 | 每個 task 都先寫測試；`tests/` 只有 fixture 層碰真 git |
| §12 封裝與安裝 | Task 17 |

**與 spec 的三處刻意收斂**（實作時照計畫走，不要「修正」回 spec 的字面）：

1. **`ahead`/`behind` 是 `Option<u32>` 不是 `u32`**（Task 2）。spec §4 的型別與同行註解互相矛盾，取註解的語意：「沒有 upstream」必須能和「與 upstream 齊平」區分。
2. **`]` / `[` 會自動展開被收合的祖先**（Task 10）。spec §5.3 定義它是「跳至下一個變更檔」，不是「下一個可見的變更檔」；不自動展開的話它在全收合的樹上是死鍵。
3. **submodule 一律從 `.gitmodules` 列舉，不受 `scan_depth` 與 `scan_excludes` 限制**（Task 7）。spec §3.2 說 submodule「不依賴遞迴走訪找到」；深度上限與硬排除是控制**走訪**成本的手段，而列舉一個已宣告的 submodule 只花一次 stat。

**兩個環境層面的偏離**（已在 Global Constraints 說明）：

- 專案路徑是 `/home/bal/project/herdr-plugin2`，不是 spec 寫的 `/home/leolu/projects/herdr-p`（後者不存在）。
- spec §3.2 舉例的目標 workspace（`teleagent` 及其 5 個 repo）在本機不存在，所以 Task 7 的 fixture **現造**一棵同形狀的樹來驗證那條載重規則。
